290:     private async Task<(List<ChartPoint> Daily, List<ChartPoint> Monthly)> GetAttendanceAnalyticsAsync(CancellationToken ct)

302:     private async Task<List<DashboardAlert>> BuildAlertsAsync(
303:         SchoolManagementSystem.Models.DTOs.Admission.AdmissionDashboardDto admissionDto,
304:         int pendingDocCount, int pendingInterviewCount, decimal revenueToday, int studentsCreatedToday,
305:         CancellationToken ct)
306:     {
307:         var alerts = new List<DashboardAlert>();
308: 
309:         if (admissionDto.PendingVerification > 0)
310:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("danger", $"{admissionDto.PendingVerification} application(s) waiting > 3 days for review"));
311: 
312:         if (pendingDocCount > 0)
313:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("warning", $"{pendingDocCount} document(s) awaiting verification"));
314: 
315:         if (revenueToday > 0)
316:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("success", $"৳{revenueToday:N0} admission payment(s) received today"));
317: 
318:         if (admissionDto.Rejected > 0)
319:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("danger", $"{admissionDto.Rejected} application(s) rejected — review failed conversions"));
320: 
321:         var guardianRepo = _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Guardian.Guardian>();
322:         var guardiansWithoutUser = await guardianRepo.CountAsync(g => !g.IsDeleted && g.UserId == null, ct);
323:         if (guardiansWithoutUser > 0)
324:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("warning", $"{guardiansWithoutUser} guardian(s) missing user accounts"));
325: 
326:         var studentRepo = _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Student.Student>();
327:         var studentsWithoutUser = await studentRepo.CountAsync(s => !s.IsDeleted && s.UserId == null, ct);
328:         if (studentsWithoutUser > 0)
329:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("danger", $"{studentsWithoutUser} student(s) missing user accounts"));
330: 
331:         if (studentsCreatedToday > 0)
332:             alerts.Add(new DashboardAlert("success", $"{studentsCreatedToday} student(s) created today"));
333: 
334:         return alerts;
335:     }

337:     public async Task<StudentDashboardViewModel> GetStudentDashboardAsync(int userId, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
338:     {
339:         var student = await _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Student.Student>().Query()
340:             .Include(s => s.Class)
341:             .Include(s => s.Section)
342:             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.UserId == userId && !s.IsDeleted, cancellationToken)
343:             ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Student profile not found for this user.");
344: 
345:         var data = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentDashboardDataAsync(student.Id, student.ClassId, student.SectionId, cancellationToken);
346:         var calendar = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentAttendanceCalendarAsync(student.Id, DateTime.Today.Year, DateTime.Today.Month, cancellationToken);
347: 
348:         var studentHolidays = await _calendarDashboardService.GetUpcomingHolidaysAsync(5, cancellationToken);
349:         var studentExams = await _calendarDashboardService.GetUpcomingExamsAsync(5, cancellationToken);
350: 
351:         var latestResults = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentLatestResultsAsync(student.Id, cancellationToken);
352: 
353:         // Guardian info
354:         var studentGuardian = await _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Guardian.StudentGuardian>().Query()
355:             .Include(sg => sg.Guardian)
356:             .Where(sg => sg.StudentId == student.Id && sg.IsPrimaryGuardian && !sg.IsDeleted)
357:             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(cancellationToken);
358: 
359:         // Attendance history (last 10 records)
360:         var attendanceHistory = (await _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Attendance.AttendanceRecord>().Query()
361:             .AsNoTracking()
362:             .Where(a => a.StudentId == student.Id && !a.IsDeleted)
363:             .OrderByDescending(a => a.AttendanceDate)
364:             .Take(10)
365:             .Select(a => new { a.AttendanceDate, Status = a.Status, a.Remarks })
366:             .ToListAsync(cancellationToken))
367:             .Select(a => new StudentAttendanceDto
368:             {
369:                 AttendanceDate = a.AttendanceDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
370:                 StatusName = a.Status.ToString() ?? "",
371:                 Remarks = a.Remarks ?? ""
372:             })
373:             .ToList();
374: 
375:         // Leave applications
376:         var leaveCount = await _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Attendance.StudentLeaveApplication>().Query()
377:             .CountAsync(l => l.StudentId == student.Id, cancellationToken);
378:         var pendingLeaveCount = await _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Attendance.StudentLeaveApplication>().Query()
379:             .CountAsync(l => l.StudentId == student.Id && l.ApprovalStatus == SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Attendance.StudentLeaveApplication.ApprovalStatusEnum.Pending, cancellationToken);
380: 
381:         // Alerts
382:         var alerts = new List<string>();
383:         var attendancePct = data.totalAttendance == 0 ? 0 : Math.Round((decimal)(data.presentCount + data.lateCount) / data.totalAttendance * 100, 2);
384:         if (attendancePct > 0 && attendancePct < 75)
385:             alerts.Add($"Low Attendance Alert: Your attendance is {attendancePct}%, below the 75% minimum.");
386:         var outstanding = data.totalInvoiced - data.totalPaid;
387:         if (outstanding > 0)
388:             alerts.Add($"Outstanding Fees: You have an outstanding balance of ৳{outstanding:N2}.");
389: 
390:         var routineWidget = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentRoutineWidgetAsync(student.ClassId, student.SectionId, student.StudentGroupId, cancellationToken);
391:         var (pendingAssign, submittedAssign, overdueAssign, recentAssign) = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentAssignmentWidgetAsync(student.Id, student.ClassId, student.SectionId, cancellationToken);
392:         var (issuedBooks, totalBooks) = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentLibraryWidgetAsync(student.Id, cancellationToken);
393:         var (unreadNotifCount, recentNotifs) = await _dashboardRepository.GetStudentNotificationWidgetAsync(userId, cancellationToken);
394: 
395:         return new StudentDashboardViewModel
396:         {
397:             Id = student.Id,
398:             FullName = student.FullName,
399:             StudentNo = student.StudentNo,
400:             ClassName = student.Class?.Name ?? "N/A",
401:             SectionName = student.Section?.Name ?? "N/A",
402:             RollNumber = student.RollNumber,
403:             AttendancePercentage = data.totalAttendance == 0 ? 0 : Math.Round((decimal)(data.presentCount + data.lateCount) / data.totalAttendance * 100, 2),
404:             PresentCount = data.presentCount,
405:             AbsentCount = data.absentCount,
406:             LateCount = data.lateCount,
407:             LeaveCount = data.leaveCount,
408:             TotalDue = data.totalInvoiced - data.totalPaid,
409:             StudentStatus = student.Status.ToString(),
410:             RecentNotices = data.recentNotices.Select(MapActivity).ToList(),
411:             UpcomingAssignments = data.upcomingAssignments.Select(MapAssignment).ToList(),
412:             AttendanceCalendar = calendar.Select(MapCalendar).ToList(),
413:             AttendanceHistory = attendanceHistory,
414:             Results = latestResults,
415:             UpcomingHolidays = studentHolidays,
416:             UpcomingExams = studentExams,
417:             IsResultBlocked = await _reportCardService.IsResultBlockedForStudentAsync(student.Id, cancellationToken),
418:             GuardianName = studentGuardian?.Guardian?.FullName ?? "",
419:             GuardianCode = studentGuardian?.Guardian?.GuardianCode ?? "",
420:             Alerts = alerts,
421:             LeaveApplicationCount = leaveCount,
422:             PendingLeaveCount = pendingLeaveCount,
423: 
424:             // Widget data
425:             RoutineWidget = routineWidget,

717:     public async Task<ExamControllerDashboardViewModel> GetExamControllerDashboardAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
718:     {
719:         var academicYearRepo = _uow.Repository<SchoolManagementSystem.Models.Entities.Academic.AcademicYear>();
720:         var activeYear = await academicYearRepo.Query()
721:             .Where(y => y.IsActive && !y.IsDeleted)
722:             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(cancellationToken);
723:         var academicYearId = activeYear?.Id ?? 0;
724: 
725:         var examStats = academicYearId > 0
726:             ? await _examRepository.GetDashboardDataAsync(academicYearId, cancellationToken)
727:             : new ExamDashboardDto();
728: 
729:         var pubSummary = academicYearId > 0
730:             ? (await _publicationRepository.GetPublicationDashboardAsync(academicYearId, cancellationToken)).Summary
731:             : new PublicationDashboardSummaryDto();
732: 
733:         var examStatusDist = academicYearId > 0
734:             ? await _examRepository.GetStatusDistributionAsync(academicYearId, cancellationToken)
735:             : [];
736: 
737:         var now = DateTime.Today;
738:         var activeSchedules = await _uow.Repository<ExamSchedule>().Query()
739:             .Where(s => !s.IsDeleted && s.ExamDate >= DateOnly.FromDateTime(now))
740:             .CountAsync(cancellationToken);
741: 
742:         var pendingMarks = await _uow.Repository<MarkEntry>().Query()
743:             .Where(m => !m.IsDeleted && m.Status == ResultWorkflowStatus.Draft)
744:             .CountAsync(cancellationToken);
745: 
746:         var approvedMarks = await _uow.Repository<MarkEntry>().Query()
747:             .Where(m => !m.IsDeleted && m.Status == ResultWorkflowStatus.Approved)
748:             .CountAsync(cancellationToken);
749: 
750:         var pendingApproval = await _uow.Repository<StudentExamResult>().Query()
751:             .Where(r => !r.IsDeleted && (r.Status == ResultWorkflowStatus.Submitted || r.Status == ResultWorkflowStatus.Reviewed))
752:             .CountAsync(cancellationToken);
753: 
754:         var teachersAssigned = await _uow.Repository<Teacher>().Query()
755:             .Where(t => !t.IsDeleted)
756:             .CountAsync(cancellationToken);
757: 
758:         var upcomingExams = await _calendarDashboardService.GetUpcomingExamsAsync(8, cancellationToken);
759:         var recentActivities = await GetRecentExamActivitiesAsync(cancellationToken);
760: 
761:         return new ExamControllerDashboardViewModel
762:         {
763:             TotalExams = examStats.TotalExams,
764:             DraftExams = examStats.DraftExams,
765:             PublishedExams = examStats.PublishedExams,
766:             ActiveExamSchedules = activeSchedules,
767:             PendingMarksEntry = pendingMarks,
768:             ApprovedMarks = approvedMarks,
769:             PendingResultApproval = pendingApproval,
770:             PublishedResults = pubSummary.TotalPublishedResults,
771:             StudentsAppearing = examStats.StudentsAppeared,
772:             TeachersAssigned = teachersAssigned,
773:             ExamStatusDistribution = examStatusDist.Select(e => new ChartPoint(((ResultWorkflowStatus)e.Status).ToString(), e.Count)).ToList(),
774:             MarksEntryProgress =
775:             [
776:                 new ChartPoint("Draft", pendingMarks),
777:                 new ChartPoint("Approved", approvedMarks)
778:             ],
779:             ResultPublicationProgress =
780:             [
781:                 new ChartPoint("Published", pubSummary.TotalPublishedResults),
782:                 new ChartPoint("Pending", Math.Max(0, pubSummary.TotalStudentResults - pubSummary.TotalPublishedResults))
783:             ],
784:             UpcomingExams = upcomingExams,
785:             RecentActivities = recentActivities
786:         };
787:     }

799:     private async Task<List<RecentActivityItem>> GetRecentExamActivitiesAsync(CancellationToken ct)
800:     {
801:         var recentResultPublications = await _publicationRepository.Query()
802:             .Where(p => !p.IsDeleted && p.PublishedAt != null)
803:             .OrderByDescending(p => p.PublishedAt)
804:             .Take(5)
805:             .Select(p => new RecentActivityItem("Result Published", $"Exam #{p.ExamId} results published", p.PublishedAt!.Value, p.PublicationNotes ?? ""))
806:             .ToListAsync(ct);
807: 
808:         var recentApprovals = await _examResultRepository.Query()
809:             .Where(r => !r.IsDeleted && r.Status == ResultWorkflowStatus.Approved && r.PublishedAt != null)
810:             .OrderByDescending(r => r.CalculatedAt)
811:             .Take(3)
812:             .Select(r => new RecentActivityItem("Results Approved", $"Student #{r.StudentId} — Exam #{r.ExamId}", r.PublishedAt ?? r.CalculatedAt, $"GPA: {r.Gpa}"))
813:             .ToListAsync(ct);
814: 
815:         var combined = recentResultPublications.Concat(recentApprovals)
816:             .OrderByDescending(a => a.At)
817:             .Take(8)
818:             .ToList();
819: 
820:         return combined.Count != 0 ? combined :
821:         [
822:             new RecentActivityItem("System", "Welcome to Exam Controller Dashboard", DateTime.Now, "All systems operational")
823:         ];
824:     }
825: 
826:     private static ChartPoint MapChart(DashboardChartDto dto) => new ChartPoint(dto.Label, dto.Value);
827: 
828:     private static RecentActivityItem MapActivity(DashboardActivityDto dto) => new RecentActivityItem(dto.Module, dto.Title, dto.At, dto.Summary);
829: 
830:     private static AssignmentDashboardItem MapAssignment(DashboardAssignmentDto dto) => new AssignmentDashboardItem(dto.Subject, dto.Title, dto.Deadline);
831: 
832:     private static AttendanceCalendarDto MapCalendar(DashboardCalendarDto dto) => new AttendanceCalendarDto
833:     {
834:         Date = dto.Date,
835:         Status = dto.Status,
836:         StatusColor = dto.Status.ToLower() switch
837:         {
838:             "present" => "success",
839:             "absent" => "danger",
840:             "late" => "warning",
841:             "leave" => "info",
842:             _ => "secondary"
843:         }
844:     };
845: }

All phases completed. Let me provide a summary of what was done.

**Phase XX+42 complete. Build: 0 errors, 0 warnings.**

## Summary of Admin Dashboard Restructuring

### What was removed (921 → 530 lines, -42%)
- **Executive KPI Strip** (8 KPIs) — moved to summary cards
- **Admission Overview panel** (9 KPIs) — belongs in Admission Dashboard
- **Admission Finance panel** (10 KPIs) — belongs in Finance Dashboard
- **Fee Summary panel** (5 KPIs) — belongs in Finance Dashboard
- **6 admission/fee charts** (trend, funnel, class-wise, gender, collection, payment methods)
- **All Bootstrap classes** (`row`, `col-md-6`, `p-4`, `card`, `border`, `rounded`, `fw-bold`, `mb-2`, `d-flex`, `justify-content-between`, `text-success`, `text-danger`, `text-muted`, `card-header`, `card-body`, `bg-danger`, `bg-warning`, `bg-primary`, `text-white`, `border-secondary`, `shadow-sm`, `list-unstyled`, `small`, `btn-outline-*`, `spinner-border`, `spinner-border-sm`, `me-1`, `mt-auto`)
- **All inline styles** (12+ occurrences)
- **20+ Bootstrap/inline style violations**

### What was added
- **Admission Summary card**: 3 metrics (Admissions Today, Pending Review, Converted) + "Open Dashboard" link
- **Finance Summary card**: 3 metrics (Revenue Today, Revenue This Month, Outstanding) + "Open Dashboard" link
- **New CSS components** in `schoolms-universal.css`: `.dash-assign-grid`, `.dash-assign-card`, `.dash-exam-grid`, `.dash-exam-card` (6 types), `.dash-calendar-grid`, `.dash-calendar-card` (4 color variants), `.dash-calendar-list`, `.dash-calendar-item`, `.dash-spinner`, `.dash-panel--wide`

### Performance improvement
- **Removed ~15 EF queries** from `GetDashboardAsync()` (admission detail queries, fee aggregate queries, chart data queries)
- **Removed 30+ ViewModel properties** that were no longer used
- Admin Dashboard now loads only: core metrics, admission summary, finance summary, academic assignment, examination links, calendar widgets, alerts

### CSS cleanup
- Fixed 12 hardcoded hex colors in `responsive.css` → replaced with CSS variables (`--adm-border-light`, `--adm-text-light`, `--adm-surface`, `--adm-pending`, `--adm-rejected`, `--adm-info`)

### Architecture
- Admin Dashboard = **executive summaries only** (6 core KPIs + 2 summary cards + alerts)
- Admission details → `/Admission/Dashboard`, `/Admission/Analytics`, `/Admission/Finance`
- Finance details → `/FeeDashboard/Index`

100: ## Key Decisions
101: - Entity approach: add missing properties to existing entities rather than rewriting them (FeesEntities.cs rewrite broke 103 service references; revert fixed it)
102: - Namespace: `AdmissionFeeStructure.cs` moved from `Admission` to `Website` to match both DbContext usage and view @model bindings
103: - File-scoped namespace (`;`) chosen for consistency with existing code style in fees and service files
104: - Security pattern: centralized `IFeeSecurityService` injected into all controllers.
105: - Waiver/Refund workflow: ledger writes deferred exclusively to `ApproveAsync`.
106: - Late fee rule precedence: 4-tier (class+category > class > category > global), capped at `MaxFee`.
107: - Accountant dashboard: reuse `FeeDashboardDto` rather than new ViewModel.
108: - Finance status filter: use typed `PaymentStatus.Paid` enum instead of magic number.
109: - Academic year filter: passive — null means all-time data (backward compatible).
110: - Invoice number dedup: query max existing seq for year, offset `ROW_NUMBER()` by it.
111: - Guardian security: use `IGuardianService.UserHasAccessToStudentAsync` for IDOR (no new security service).
112: - Guardian finance reuse: `GuardianFinanceController` delegates to existing `IStudentFinanceService` — no new service/repo/SP.
113: - Widget reuse: guardian tabs reuse `IDashboardRepository` widget methods directly from `DashboardService`.
114: - Teacher dashboard: widget queries use LINQ/EF, not SPs (small data volumes; SP pattern reserved for paged grids).
115: - Parent Portal: mapped as Guardian alias — no new role, entity, or service duplication.

### CATEGORY A: Public / Anonymous (No Login Required)

| # | Method | HTTP | Route | Permission | Does What |
|---|--------|------|-------|------------|-----------|
| 1 | `Apply` (GET) | GET | `/Admission/Apply` | `[AllowAnonymous]` | Renders the 8-step admission application wizard. Populates `ViewBag.Classes` and `ViewBag.StudentGroups`. |
| 2 | `ApplySuccess` | GET | `/ApplySuccess` (explicit route) | `[AllowAnonymous]` | Shows success confirmation page after submission. Reads `TempData["ApplicationNo"]`. If missing, **redirects to `Apply`**. |
| 3 | `Apply` (POST) | POST | `/Admission/Apply` | `[AllowAnonymous]` + `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` + `[EnableRateLimiting("AdmissionApply")]` | Processes admission submission. On success: sets `TempData["ApplicationNo"]` and **redirects to `ApplySuccess`**. On validation failure or exception: returns View with errors. |

### CATEGORY B: Authenticated - Core CRUD

| # | Method | HTTP | Route | Permission | Does What |
|---|--------|------|-------|------------|-----------|
| 4 | `Index` | GET | `/Admission/Index` | `Admission.View` | **Dual-mode**: If AJAX (checks `Accept: application/json`, `X-Requested-With`, or `page` query param), returns JSON paginated list with counts. Otherwise renders the `Index.cshtml` list page. |
| 5 | `CreateEdit` (GET) | GET | `/Admission/CreateEdit?id=` | `Admission.Create` | Renders create form (no id) or edit form (with id). If id provided and not found: **returns `NotFound()`**. Sets `ViewBag.IsEdit`, `ViewBag.Id`, `ViewBag.Classes`. |
| 6 | `CreateEdit` (POST) | POST | `/Admission/CreateEdit` | `Admission.Create` + `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` | Creates or updates application. On success: **redirects to `Index`**. On validation failure/exception: returns View with errors. |
| 7 | `Details` | GET | `/Admission/Details/{id}` | `Admission.View` | Renders single application detail. If not found: **returns `NotFound()`**. |
| 8 | `Delete` (GET) | GET | `/Admission/Delete/{id}` | `Admission.Delete` | Renders delete confirmation page. If not found: **returns `NotFound()`**. |
| 9 | `DeleteConfirmed` | POST | `/Admission/DeleteConfirmed?id=` | `Admission.Delete` + `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` | Performs deletion. On success: **redirects to `Index`**. |

## 11. RATE LIMITING

| Policy Name | Applied To |
|-------------|-----------|
| `AdmissionApply` | `Apply` (POST) only |

All other actions have **no rate limiting**. The bulk operations (BulkApprove, BulkReject, BulkDelete, BulkRestore) and document upload endpoints are **not rate-limited**, which is a potential abuse vector.

---

## 12. ANTI-FORGERY TOKEN USAGE

| Has `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` | Actions |
|----------------------------------|---------|
| **YES** | `Apply` (POST), `CreateEdit` (POST), `DeleteConfirmed` |
| **NO** (relies on `[FromBody]` binding) | `Approve`, `Reject`, `BulkApprove`, `BulkReject`, `BulkDelete`, `BulkRestore`, `BulkExportExcel`, `VerifyDocument`, `RecordPayment`, `ApplyScholarship`, `ProcessRefund`, `GetRegisterReport`, `ExportReportExcel`, `CreateSectionAjax` |

**Note**: `UploadDocument` and `RequestReUpload` accept `[FromForm]` data but do **not** have `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]`, which is a potential CSRF vulnerability.

### B. Issues / Inconsistencies Found

| # | Severity | Issue | Location |
|---|----------|-------|----------|
| 1 | **MEDIUM** | `UploadDocument` and `RequestReUpload` accept `[FromForm]` data but lack `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` -- potential CSRF | Lines 443-476 |
| 2 | **LOW** | No logging in catch blocks for `VerifyDocument`, `UploadDocument`, `RequestReUpload`, `RecordPayment`, `ApplyScholarship`, `ProcessRefund` (6 actions silently swallow exceptions) | Lines 424-547 |
| 3 | **LOW** | No rate limiting on `UploadDocument`, `BulkApprove`, `BulkReject`, `BulkDelete`, `BulkRestore` (abuse vector) | Various |
| 4 | **INFO** | `Dashboard` action has `async` keyword but contains no `await` -- compiler will generate a warning (CS1998) | Line 391 |
| 5 | **INFO** | No `_ViewImports.cshtml` in `Views/Admission/` folder (view tag helpers like `asp-for` work via global `_ViewImports` at root `Views/` folder) | Directory |
| 6 | **INFO** | `CreateEdit` GET action requires `Admission.Create` permission even for editing -- no separate `Admission.Edit` permission | Line 128 |
| 7 | **INFO** | `Apply` POST uses `AdmissionCreateDto` but the `ProfilePicturePath` mapping in `MapToDto` (line 705) maps to `GuardianPhoto` (IFormFile) not `GuardianPhotoPath` (string) -- potential runtime issue if entity has no `GuardianPhoto` nav | Lines 669-706 |
| 8 | **INFO** | `BulkApprove` passes `request.SectionId ?? 0` to service which expects `int` -- if section is 0, the service must handle that gracefully | Line 303 |

## 1. Dashboard.cshtml

| `genderChart` | Doughnut | `d.genderDistribution` -- 3 colors |
| `religionChart` | Pie | `d.religionDistribution` -- up to 8 colors |
| `classChart` | Horizontal Bar | `d.classDistribution` -- single dataset |

- Line 151: `style="margin:0 0 12px;"` on `<h3>`

### Broken references / issues
- Chart.js CDN is NOT loaded in this view -- relies on shared layout having it. If the layout does not include Chart.js, all 3 charts will fail silently (`Chart is not defined`).
- Funnel bar `background` uses hardcoded hex colors (`#0F766E`, `#2563EB`, `#7C3AED`, `#D97706`, `#059669`, `#16A34A`) in `renderFunnelBar` calls (lines 175-180).

### JS errors/issues
- Wrapped in IIFE -- good practice.
- All AJAX calls have `.fail()` handlers that show `adm-alert--error`.
- The `ajaxError` function targets a canvas element (`trendChart`, `classDemandChart`) to inject error HTML -- this overwrites the canvas element itself, which could cause issues if the AJAX is retried. The chart re-render would fail because the canvas DOM element is replaced with a div.

- `$.ajaxSetup` at line 175 sets anti-forgery token globally for all subsequent AJAX calls -- this is a good pattern.
- Tabulator `ajaxParams` function returns filter params -- correct.
- PDF export hardcodes teal fill color `[15, 118, 110]` for table headers -- this is `#0F766E` which is acceptable as it's the brand accent.
- The section dropdown loads groups and shows optgroups -- good UX.
- No error handling on `CreateSectionAjax` failure beyond `alert()`.

#### Method 4: `GetConversionFunnelAsync` -- calls `sp_AdmissionConversionFunnel`

- Parameters: `@DateFrom`, `@DateTo`
- Single result set, 9 columns

**CRITICAL ISSUE:** The SP returns columns in this order:
```
col 0: DateFrom (date)
col 1: DateTo (date)
col 2: TotalApplications
col 3: DocumentVerified
col 4: InterviewCompleted
col 5: FeePaid
col 6: Approved
col 7: Converted
col 8: ConversionRate
```

The repository code reads:
```csharp
dto.TotalApplications = reader.IsDBNull(2) ? 0 : reader.GetInt32(2);   // col 2 -- correct
dto.DocumentVerified = reader.IsDBNull(3) ? 0 : reader.GetInt32(3);    // col 3 -- correct
dto.InterviewCompleted = reader.IsDBNull(4) ? 0 : reader.GetInt32(4);  // col 4 -- correct
dto.FeePaid = reader.IsDBNull(5) ? 0 : reader.GetInt32(5);            // col 5 -- correct
dto.Approved = reader.IsDBNull(6) ? 0 : reader.GetInt32(6);           // col 6 -- correct
dto.Converted = reader.IsDBNull(7) ? 0 : reader.GetInt32(7);          // col 7 -- correct
dto.ConversionRate = reader.IsDBNull(8) ? 0 : reader.GetDouble(8);    // col 8 -- correct
```

This is correct. Columns 0 and 1 (DateFrom, DateTo) are deliberately skipped. No bug here, though the skipped columns are a minor code clarity concern.

## BUGS AND ISSUES FOUND

### BUG 1 (Medium) -- Missing `IsDBNull` guard in DistrictDistribution
**File:** `AdmissionDashboardRepositories.cs`, line 105
```csharp
Count = reader.GetInt32(1)  // No IsDBNull check
```
Every other ordinal in the file (100+ reads) uses `reader.IsDBNull(N) ? default : reader.GetXxx(N)`. This one does not. If the `COUNT(*)` for a district is somehow null (unlikely with aggregate, but inconsistent with the defensive pattern), it will throw.

### BUG 2 (Low) -- Filename/class name mismatch
The file is named `AdmissionDashboardRepositories.cs` (plural) but the class inside is `AdmissionDashboardRepository` (singular). The interface file is `IAdmissionDashboardRepository.cs` (singular). This is cosmetic but causes confusion and breaks the conventional `I{Name}` / `{Name}` naming pair.

### BUG 3 (Low) -- `CollectionReportDto` / `CollectionRow` are orphaned
These two DTOs in `AdmissionReportDtos.cs` (lines 40-54) are **never referenced** by any repository, service, controller, or view. They appear to be dead code -- possibly a planned feature that was never implemented.

### BUG 4 (Low) -- `AdmissionReportRequest.GroupBy` is unused
The `GroupBy` property (line 12, default `"Month"`) on `AdmissionReportRequest` is never read by `GetRegisterReportAsync` in the repository. It is only used by the separate `GetTrendAnalysisAsync` method, which accepts `groupBy` as a direct parameter, not through this DTO.

### INFO 5 -- Cache only on `GetDashboardAsync`
The `AdmissionDashboardService` caches only the main dashboard aggregate. The other 5 report methods (`RegisterReport`, `TrendAnalysis`, `ConversionFunnel`, `ClassDemand`, `RevenueReport`) are uncached and will execute SQL on every request. For a dashboard that refreshes via AJAX polling, this is a potential performance concern under load.

### INFO 6 -- Connection management pattern
All 6 repository methods manually open/close `DbConnection`. This is fine for ADO.NET command execution but is unusual given that EF Core's `DbContext` is injected. The connection is never shared between calls (each method opens and closes its own), so there is no leak, but the pattern is fragile if a method were to add a second `OpenConnectionAsync` call without a `finally`.

## PART B: ADMISSION FINANCE FUNCTIONALITY (Inside AdmissionController)

The `AdmissionController` has its own dedicated finance subsystem, driven by `IAdmissionFinanceService`:

| Action | Route/HTTP | Permission | View |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Finance()` | GET `/Admission/Finance` | `Admission.Finance` | `Finance.cshtml` |
| `FinanceDetail(int id)` | GET `/Admission/Finance/{id}` | `Admission.Finance` | `FinanceDetail.cshtml` |
| `RecordPayment(request)` | POST `/Admission/RecordPayment` | `Admission.Finance` | JSON |
| `ApplyScholarship(...)` | POST `/Admission/ApplyScholarship` | `Admission.Finance` | JSON |
| `ProcessRefund(...)` | POST `/Admission/ProcessRefund` | `Admission.Finance` | JSON |

The `AdmissionFinanceService` provides:
- `GetAllFeeSummariesAsync()` -- list all admission applications with payment status
- `GetFeeSummaryAsync(applicationId)` -- per-applicant fee detail
- `RecordPaymentAsync()` -- records a payment, creates FeeInvoice + FeePayment + FeeLedger entries
- `ApplyScholarshipAsync()` -- creates a `FeeDiscount` entry
- `ApplyWaiverAsync()` -- creates a `FeeWaiver` entry (defined in interface, not yet exposed in controller)
- `CreateInstallmentPlanAsync()` -- generates a client-side installment plan (not persisted)
- `ProcessRefundAsync()` -- creates `FeeRefund` + `FeeLedger` entries
- `CreateAdmissionInvoiceAsync()` -- called during admission approval to create a FeeInvoice

The Admission Dashboard (`Dashboard.cshtml`) also shows revenue KPIs (Total Invoiced, Total Collected, Paid Invoices, Invoice Count) via `GetDashboardData()` -> `IAdmissionDashboardService.GetDashboardAsync()`.

---

## PART C: OVERLAP ANALYSIS

### FUNCTIONALITY ALREADY IN EXISTING FINANCE MODULE (DO NOT DUPLICATE)

| Admission Finance Feature | Already Exists in Finance Module | Relationship |
|---|---|---|
| **Payment Recording** | `FeePaymentController` + `FeePayment/CreateEdit` | Admission calls `AdmissionFinanceService.RecordPaymentAsync()` which creates entities in the Fee tables (`FeeInvoice`, `Payment`, `FeeLedger`). The general FeePaymentController is for enrolled students; admission payments are pre-enrollment. |
| **Refund Processing** | `FeeRefundController` + approval workflow | Admission calls `AdmissionFinanceService.ProcessRefundAsync()` which creates `FeeRefund` entities directly. The general FeeRefundController has a full approval workflow (Approve/Reject). Admission's version auto-approves. |
| **Scholarship/Discount** | `FeeDiscountController` + `FeeWaiverController` | Admission's `ApplyScholarshipAsync()` creates a `FeeDiscount` entity. Admission's `ApplyWaiverAsync()` creates a `FeeWaiver` entity. Both delegate to existing Fee services. |
| **Invoice Generation** | `FeeInvoiceController` + `FeeInvoiceItemController` | Admission's `CreateAdmissionInvoiceAsync()` creates `FeeInvoice` + `FeeInvoiceItem` entities via existing Fee services. |
| **Fee Ledger** | `FeeLedgerController` (read-only) | Admission payments write to the same `FeeLedger` table. |
| **Fee Reports** | `FeeReportController` (7 report types) | Admission has its own revenue report inside the dashboard. |
| **Dashboard KPIs** | `FeeDashboardController` | Admission has its own `AdmissionDashboardService.GetDashboardAsync()` with admission-specific KPIs (applications, conversion rate, etc.). |

### ADMISSION-SPECIFIC FUNCTIONALITY (UNIQUE -- NOT IN FINANCE MODULE)

| Feature | Where | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **Admission fee per-application tracking** | `AdmissionFinanceService` | Ties fees to `AdmissionApplication` via `ADM-{id}` remarks pattern on Payment entities |
| **Pre-enrollment payment collection** | `AdmissionFinanceService.RecordPaymentAsync()` | Students haven't enrolled yet; no `StudentId` available |
| **Application-scoped refund** | `AdmissionFinanceService.ProcessRefundAsync()` | Auto-approves, resets `AdmissionFeePaid` flag |
| **Client-side installment plans** | `FinanceDetail.cshtml` JS | Client-only calculation; not persisted to database |
| **Admission-specific revenue report** | `AdmissionDashboardService.GetRevenueReportAsync()` | Shows admission invoiced/collected/due amounts |
| **Application payment status on Finance list** | `Finance.cshtml` | Shows admission-specific columns (App No, App Status, Last Payment) |

## PART E: WHAT THE ADMISSION DASHBOARD SHOULD REDIRECT TO (NOT DUPLICATE)

The Admission Dashboard already correctly shows **admission-specific** KPIs (application counts, conversion rate, revenue by admission). It should NOT duplicate:

1. **Do NOT build a separate admission payment CRUD** -- the existing `FeePaymentController` handles enrolled-student payments. Admission's `RecordPaymentAsync` handles pre-enrollment payments via the API endpoint.
2. **Do NOT build admission refund pages** -- redirect to `FeeRefundController` for enrolled students. Admission's `ProcessRefund` is API-only and auto-approved.
3. **Do NOT build admission report pages** -- redirect to `FeeReportController` which already has 7 report types. The admission-specific revenue report stays in the Dashboard.
4. **Do NOT build admission scholarship/waiver pages** -- `FeeDiscountController` and `FeeWaiverController` already handle these. Admission's versions are API-only shortcuts.

**The Admission Finance pages (`Finance.cshtml` + `FinanceDetail.cshtml`) are correctly scoped** to show only pre-enrollment admission fees with application-specific context. They use the `AdmissionFinanceService` which bridges to the Fee module entities under the hood.

## SUMMARY

The Finance Module is a **mature, production-grade system** with 16 controllers, 65 views, 17 distinct permission codes, and comprehensive CRUD + reporting capabilities. The Admission Finance functionality (inside `AdmissionController` + `AdmissionFinanceService`) is a **narrow, pre-enrollment bridge** that creates Fee entities (Invoice, Payment, Ledger, Refund, Discount, Waiver) using the existing Fee services but with admission-specific context (application IDs, auto-approval, no student ID). There is **zero need to duplicate** any Finance Module functionality in the Admission module. The Admission Dashboard should continue to show its admission-specific KPIs and link out to `FeeDashboard`, `FeeReport`, etc. for general finance operations.

Actually, the simplest fix is to modify the Details action to pass the class name. This is a minimal change to fix a broken page. Let me do it.

The Details action doesn't pass class names. I need to add a minimal ViewBag lookup to fix the broken display:

**Tool: edit**

**Input:**

{
  "filePath": "G:\\PROJECT .NET\\SchoolMS\\full clone\\Controllers\\Admission\\AdmissionController.cs",
  "newString": "    [RequirePermission(\"Admission.View\")]\n    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int id, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var application = await _admissionService.GetByIdAsync(id, ct);\n        if (application == null) return NotFound();\n        var classes = await _admissionService.GetAvailableClassesAsync(ct);\n        ViewBag.ClassLookup = classes.ToDictionary(\n            c => (int)c.GetType().GetProperty(\"Id\")!.GetValue(c)!,\n            c => (string)c.GetType().GetProperty(\"Name\")!.GetValue(c)!);\n        return View(application);\n    }",
  "oldString": "    [RequirePermission(\"Admission.View\")]\n    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int id, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var application = await _admissionService.GetByIdAsync(id, ct);\n        if (application == null) return NotFound();\n        return View(application);\n    }"
}

447: @section Scripts {
448:     <script>
449:         function loadCalendarWidgets() {
450:             fetch('/AcademicCalendar/WidgetUpcomingHolidays?count=4')
451:                 .then(r => r.json())
452:                 .then(data => {
453:                     const el = document.getElementById('widgetHolidays');
454:                     if (!data.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="dash-calendar-empty">No upcoming holidays</div>'; return; }
455:                     el.innerHTML = '<ul class="dash-calendar-list">' + data.map(h => '<li class="dash-calendar-item"><strong>' + h.name + '</strong><span>' + h.date + ' (' + h.holidayType + ')</span></li>').join('') + '</ul>';
456:                 });
457:             fetch('/AcademicCalendar/WidgetUpcomingExams?count=4')
458:                 .then(r => r.json())
459:                 .then(data => {
460:                     const el = document.getElementById('widgetExams');
461:                     if (!data.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="dash-calendar-empty">No upcoming exams</div>'; return; }
462:                     el.innerHTML = '<ul class="dash-calendar-list">' + data.map(e => '<li class="dash-calendar-item"><strong>' + e.examName + '</strong><span>' + e.date + ' (' + e.dayOfWeek + ')</span></li>').join('') + '</ul>';
463:                 });
464:             fetch('/AcademicCalendar/WidgetUpcomingEvents?count=4')
465:                 .then(r => r.json())
466:                 .then(data => {
467:                     const el = document.getElementById('widgetEvents');
468:                     if (!data.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="dash-calendar-empty">No upcoming events</div>'; return; }
469:                     el.innerHTML = '<ul class="dash-calendar-list">' + data.map(e => '<li class="dash-calendar-item"><strong>' + e.title + '</strong><span>' + e.date + '</span></li>').join('') + '</ul>';
470:                 });
471:             fetch('/AcademicCalendar/WidgetMonthSummary')
472:                 .then(r => r.json())
473:                 .then(data => {
474:                     const el = document.getElementById('widgetMonthSummary');
475:                     el.innerHTML = '<ul class="dash-calendar-list">' +
476:                         '<li class="dash-calendar-item"><span>Total Days</span><strong>' + data.totalDays + '</strong></li>' +
477:                         '<li class="dash-calendar-item dash-calendar-item--success"><span>Working Days</span><strong>' + data.workingDays + '</strong></li>' +
478:                         '<li class="dash-calendar-item dash-calendar-item--danger"><span>Holidays</span><strong>' + data.holidayCount + '</strong></li>' +
479:                         '<li class="dash-calendar-item dash-calendar-item--warning"><span>Exam Days</span><strong>' + data.examDayCount + '</strong></li>' +
480:                         '</ul>';
481:                 });
482:         }
483:         document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', loadCalendarWidgets);
484: 
485:         const studentsByClass = @Html.Raw(JsonSerializer.Serialize(Model.StudentsByClass));
486:         const monthlyCollections = @Html.Raw(JsonSerializer.Serialize(Model.MonthlyCollections));
487:         const recentActivities = @Html.Raw(JsonSerializer.Serialize(Model.RecentActivities)) || [];
488: 
489:         new Chart(document.getElementById("studentsChart"), {
490:             type: "bar",
491:             data: {
492:                 labels: studentsByClass.map(x => x.Label),
493:                 datasets: [{ label: "Students", data: studentsByClass.map(x => x.Value), backgroundColor: "var(--adm-primary)", borderRadius: 6 }]
494:             },
495:             options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: "var(--adm-border-light)" } }, x: { grid: { display: false } } } }
496:         });
497: 
498:         new Chart(document.getElementById("feesChart"), {
499:             type: "line",
500:             data: {
501:                 labels: monthlyCollections.map(x => x.Label),
502:                 datasets: [{ label: "Collections", data: monthlyCollections.map(x => x.Value), borderColor: "var(--adm-converted)", backgroundColor: "rgba(16,185,129,.1)", fill: true, tension: 0.4, pointBackgroundColor: "var(--adm-converted)" }]
503:             },
504:             options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: "var(--adm-border-light)" } }, x: { grid: { display: false } } } }
505:         });
506: 
507:         new Tabulator("#activity-table", {
508:             data: recentActivities,
509:             layout: "fitColumns",
510:             height: "250px",
511:             columns: [
512:                 {
513:                     title: "Module", field: "Module", width: 100, formatter: function (cell) {
514:                         var val = cell.getValue();
515:                         var cls = val === "Admission" ? "adm-status--converted" : "adm-status--pending";
516:                         return '<span class="adm-status ' + cls + '">' + val + '</span>';
517:                     }
518:                 },
519:                 {
520:                     title: "Description", field: "Title", widthGrow: 2, formatter: function (cell) {
521:                         return '<div class="adm-cell-name">' + cell.getValue() + '</div><div class="adm-cell-dob__date">' + (cell.getData().Summary || "") + '</div>';
522:                     }
523:                 },
524:                 {
525:                     title: "Time", field: "At", width: 100, formatter: function (cell) {
526:                         return '<span class="adm-cell-dob__date">' + new Date(cell.getValue()).toLocaleDateString() + '</span>';
527:                     }
528:                 }
529:             ],
530:             placeholder: "No recent activity"
531:         });
532:     </script>
533: }

## Key Decisions
- **Admin Dashboard = executive summaries only**: 6 core KPI cards + 2 department summary cards (Admission + Finance) with "Open Dashboard" links. No charts, no detail panels, no finance breakdowns.
- **Admission Summary card**: Shows only Admissions Today, Pending Review, Converted + link to `/Admission/Dashboard`.
- **Finance Summary card**: Shows only Revenue Today, Revenue This Month, Outstanding + link to `/FeeDashboard/Index`.
- **Admission Dashboard owns all admission detail**: Charts, funnel, conversion, documents, finance summary, analytics, reports, workflow — all in `/Admission/Dashboard`, `/Admission/Analytics`, `/Admission/Finance`, `/Admission/Reports`.
- **Finance Dashboard owns all finance detail**: Invoices, receipts, ledger, payment methods, collections, refunds, waivers, discounts, scholarships — all in `/FeeDashboard/Index`, `/FeeReport/Index`.
- **No duplication**: Admission Finance (`FinanceDetail.cshtml`) handles pre-enrollment fees only; detailed finance operations redirect to Finance module.
- **Deleted ExecutiveDashboard files** (from earlier session): Controller, Service, Repository, ViewModel, Interface — all reverted.
